## Constants

Bytes and words the code reads directly (see calibration.md). * marks a structural name. Value is
the raw value; Physical applies the unit. For option bytes the bits tested are listed, with the
state in this image (1 = set).

| Address | Name | Category | Size | Value | Physical | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| $0305 | ChecksumTarget | Diagnostics | 1 | $90 (144) |  | Checksum check (Sub_2191E, P0601): value the calculated checksum must equal |
| $0315 | ObdStandardIndex | Diagnostics | 1 | $07 (7) |  | Index into the OBD-requirements value list at $32178 sent for mode $01 PID $1C (7 selects $01, OBD II as defined by CARB) |
| $0316 | CalTypeCode | Startup | 1 | $07 (7) |  | Header byte (7): copied to $0059,Z at reset, plus 4 when it is 5 or more and Options032E bit 7 is set (no reader of $0059 found) |
| $0320 | BusMsgAcData | Other | 2 | $0BC0 (3008) |  | Data word sent in the bus message $AC (Sub_3060E; bit 3 set with Options032E bit 7) |
| $0322 | BusMsgEcData | Other | 1 | $A4 (164) |  | Data byte sent in the bus message $EC (Sub_30972; low two bits replaced) |
| $0323 | BusInfoByte1 | Other | 1 | $01 (1) |  | First data byte of the bus information message built by Sub_31860 (after $06 $ED) |
| $0324 | BusInfoByte2 | Other | 1 | $14 (20) |  | Second data byte of the bus information message |
| $0325 | BusInfoByte3 | Other | 1 | $44 (68) |  | Third data byte of the bus information message (also read by the per-event code) |
| $0326 | BusInfoByte4 | Other | 1 | $28 (40) |  | Fourth data byte of the bus information message |
| $0327 | BusInfoByte5 | Other | 1 | $0C (12) |  | Fifth data byte of the bus information message (bit 6 added with calibration bit $032E.7) |
| $0328 | Options0328 | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=0, $02=0. |
| $032A | Options032A | Options | 1 | $80 (128) |  | Option byte; bit tests in the reference-event and spark code (bits 7-6: spark cap and ignition variants) |
| $032B | Options032B | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $20 SparkCalcHookOn=0, $40 DwellBatteryOn=0, $80=0. |
| $032C | Options032C | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $40 FlexFuelOn=0, $80=0. |
| $032D | Options032D | Options | 1 | $20 (32) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $02=0, $04=0, $08=0, $10=0, $18=0, $20 AcClutchLogicOn=1, $40=0, $80=0. |
| $032E | Options032E | Options | 1 | $40 (64) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $04=0, $08=0, $10=0, $20=0, $40 ManualTransmission=1, $80=0. |
| $032F | Options032F | Options | 1 | $A4 (164) |  | Option byte; bit tests in the event, fuel and closed-loop code (bits 2-1: output timing set-up) |
| $0330 | Options0330 | Options | 1 | $28 (40) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01 BattTempModelOn=0, $02=0, $04 BattTempCurveAlt=0, $08=1, $10=0, $20 TipInAirTempCheck=1. |
| $0331 | Options0331 | Options | 1 | $28 (40) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $08=1, $10=0, $20=1, $40=0, $80 AcPressureSensor=0. |
| $0332 | Options0332 | Options | 1 | $01 (1) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=1, $02=0, $04=0, $08=0, $10=0, $18=0. |
| $0333 | Options0333 | Options | 1 | $82 (130) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=0, $02=1, $04=0, $10=0, $20=0, $40=0, $80=1. |
| $0334 | Options0334 | Options | 1 | $82 (130) |  | Option byte (bit 4: output-compare set-up at reset and in the PIT code) |
| $0335 | Options0335 | Options | 1 | $08 (8) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $02=0, $04 EgrSparkScaled=0, $08=1, $0C=1, $10=0, $20=0, $40=0, $80=0. |
| $0337 | Options0337 | Options | 1 | $14 (20) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01 IdleDipKickMode=0, $02=0, $04=1, $08=0, $10 SurgeSparkOn=1, $20 SpeedLimitOn=0, $40 SpeedDampOn=0, $80 EventSparkOn=0. |
| $0338 | Options0338 | Options | 1 | $30 (48) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $02 FieldControlAlt=0, $04 FuelEventOffsetAlt=0, $08=0, $10=1, $20=1, $40=0, $80=0. |

| $033C | Options033C | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $04=0, $08=0, $10=0, $20=0, $40=0. |
| $033D | Options033D | Options | 1 | $00 (0) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $02=0, $04=0, $08 IacCoastOff=0, $10=0, $20=0. |
| $033E | Options033E | Options | 1 | $B7 (183) |  | Option byte copied to $06B8,Z at reset; bit 1 selects branches in the start and closed-loop code |
| $033F | Options033F | Options | 1 | $A8 (168) |  | Option byte copied to $06B9,Z at reset; bits 7-4 enable monitor branches in Sub_20E1E and Sub_28D78 |
| $0340 | Options0340 | Options | 1 | $9F (159) |  | Option byte (global configuration); bits used are listed with their names where known Bits: $01=1, $02=1, $04=1, $08=1, $10=1, $20=0, $40=0, $80=1. |

| $ABBA | RunModeCountRunning | Startup | 1 | $10 (16) |  | Run-mode switch: count needed once the engine has run ($0C33,Z bit 5) and the battery is at or above 11.0 V |
| $ABBB | RunModeCount | Startup | 1 | $18 (24) |  | Run-mode switch: count $0ABC,Z needed (battery at or above 11.0 V, or warm) |
| $ABBC | RunModeCountColdLowVolts | Startup | 1 | $45 (69) |  | Run-mode switch: count needed cold with the battery below 11.0 V |
| $ABD7 | FuelEventCylFactor | Fuel | 1 | $03 (3) |  | Per-cylinder offset factor (multiplied by the cylinder index $0C46,Z) |
| $ABE4 | RpmRateDivisor | Sensors | 2 | $0780 (1920) |  | Divisor of the rpm rate terms ($030C,Z, $0D9A,Z): rpm change × segment rpm / this value (word) |
